+void rowPartition(size_t n, int rank, int ranks, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    first = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+}
+
+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+        size_t first, rows;
+        rowPartition(n, r, ranks, first, rows);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * rowWidth);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows;
+    rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, datatype, buffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks, MPI_FLOAT);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+void rowPartition(size_t n, int rank, int ranks, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    first = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+}
+
+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+        size_t first, rows;
+        rowPartition(n, r, ranks, first, rows);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * rowWidth);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows;
+    rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, datatype, buffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks, MPI_FLOAT);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks, MPI_FLOAT);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+void rowPartition(size_t n, int rank, int ranks, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);

+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+        size_t first, rows;
+        rowPartition(n, r, ranks, first, rows);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * rowWidth);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows;
+    rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, datatype, buffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}

+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks, MPI_FLOAT);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));

+// MPI row decomposition or OpenMP scheduling.
+static uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t i, uint32_t j, uint32_t ray, uint32_t component) {
+    uint32_t x = 42u ^ (i * 0x9e3779b9u) ^ (j * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (ray * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ (component * 0x27d4eb2du);
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352du; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bu; x ^= x >> 16;
+    return x;
+}

+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }

+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {

+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);

+// Stateless sampling makes every (i,j,ray) reproducible regardless of the
+// MPI row decomposition or OpenMP scheduling.
+static uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t i, uint32_t j, uint32_t ray, uint32_t component) {
+    uint32_t x = 42u ^ (i * 0x9e3779b9u) ^ (j * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (ray * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ (component * 0x27d4eb2du);
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352du; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bu; x ^= x >> 16;
+    return x;
+}

+static val_t sampleUniform(uint32_t i, uint32_t j, uint32_t ray, uint32_t component) {
+    return static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(i, j, ray, component) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);

+void rowPartition(size_t n, int rank, int ranks, size_t& first, size_t& count) {

+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));

+// Stateless sampling makes every (i,j,ray) reproducible regardless of the
+// MPI row decomposition or OpenMP scheduling.
+static uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t i, uint32_t j, uint32_t ray, uint32_t component) {
+    uint32_t x = 42u ^ (i * 0x9e3779b9u) ^ (j * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (ray * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ (component * 0x27d4eb2du);
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352du; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bu; x ^= x >> 16;
+    return x;
+}

+void rowPartition(size_t n, int rank, int ranks, size_t& first, size_t& count) {

+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}

+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {

+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {

         }

+// Stateless sampling makes every (i,j,ray) reproducible regardless of the
+// MPI row decomposition or OpenMP scheduling.
+static uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t i, uint32_t j, uint32_t ray, uint32_t component) {
+    uint32_t x = 42u ^ (i * 0x9e3779b9u) ^ (j * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (ray * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ (component * 0x27d4eb2du);
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352du; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bu; x ^= x >> 16;
+    return x;
+}
+
+static val_t sampleUniform(uint32_t i, uint32_t j, uint32_t ray, uint32_t component) {
+    return static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(i, j, ray, component) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);

+void rowPartition(size_t n, int rank, int ranks, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);

+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+        size_t first, rows;
+        rowPartition(n, r, ranks, first, rows);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * rowWidth);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows;
+    rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, datatype, buffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks, MPI_FLOAT);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));

+static uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t i, uint32_t j, uint32_t ray, uint32_t component) {
+    uint32_t x = 42u ^ (i * 0x9e3779b9u) ^ (j * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (ray * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ (component * 0x27d4eb2du);

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}

+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks, MPI_FLOAT);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));

+static uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t i, uint32_t j, uint32_t ray, uint32_t component) {
+    uint32_t x = 42u ^ (i * 0x9e3779b9u) ^ (j * 0x85ebca6bu) ^

+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}

-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks, MPI_FLOAT);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+// Stateless sampling makes every (i,j,ray) reproducible regardless of the
+// MPI row decomposition or OpenMP scheduling.
+static uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t i, uint32_t j, uint32_t ray, uint32_t component) {
+    uint32_t x = 42u ^ (i * 0x9e3779b9u) ^ (j * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (ray * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ (component * 0x27d4eb2du);
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352du; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bu; x ^= x >> 16;
+    return x;
+}
+

+void rowPartition(size_t n, int rank, int ranks, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    first = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);

+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+        size_t first, rows;
+        rowPartition(n, r, ranks, first, rows);

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}

+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks, MPI_FLOAT);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherDeviceFloatRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+void rowPartition(size_t n, int rank, int ranks, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);

+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherDeviceFloatRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

codex
The two-rank validation exposed that the available MPI build is not CUDA-aware: it faults when handed device pointers. I’ve switched only the collective boundary to pinned host staging, retaining CUDA for the numerical kernels and MPI for the distributed ownership; this is required for the program to run correctly with standard MPI installations.
exec
/bin/bash -lc 'builddir=$(mktemp -d) && cmake -S roomsim -B "$builddir" >/dev/null && cmake --build "$builddir" -j 2 >/dev/null && mpirun --allow-run-as-root -np 2 env OMP_NUM_THREADS=2 "$builddir/roomsim" -n 20 -t 4 -v' in /home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-low_hybrid_r2

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherDeviceFloatRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+void gatherDeviceFloatRows(val_t* deviceBuffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}

-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherDeviceFloatRows(dRadB + t * n, 1, n, rank, ranks);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows;
+    rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, datatype, buffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+// Stage collectives through page-locked host memory.  This keeps the program
+// portable across standard MPI installations while CUDA still owns all dense
+// arithmetic; accelerator-aware MPI implementations can map this buffer for
+// their fast host/device transfer path.
+void gatherDeviceFloatRows(val_t* deviceBuffer, val_t* hostBuffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+        size_t first, rows; rowPartition(n, r, ranks, first, rows);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * rowWidth);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostBuffer + first * rowWidth, deviceBuffer + first * rowWidth,
+                          rows * rowWidth * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    MPI_Allgatherv(hostBuffer + first * rowWidth, counts[rank], MPI_FLOAT,
+                   hostBuffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceBuffer, hostBuffer, n * rowWidth * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+}

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    val_t* exchangeBuffer = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&exchangeBuffer, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherDeviceFloatRows(dRadB + t * n, exchangeBuffer, 1, n, rank, ranks);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(exchangeBuffer));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+void rowPartition(size_t n, int rank, int ranks, size_t& first, size_t& count) {

+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows;
+    rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, datatype, buffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+// Stage collectives through page-locked host memory.  This keeps the program
+// portable across standard MPI installations while CUDA still owns all dense
+// arithmetic; accelerator-aware MPI implementations can map this buffer for
+// their fast host/device transfer path.
+void gatherDeviceFloatRows(val_t* deviceBuffer, val_t* hostBuffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+        size_t first, rows; rowPartition(n, r, ranks, first, rows);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * rowWidth);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostBuffer + first * rowWidth, deviceBuffer + first * rowWidth,
+                          rows * rowWidth * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    MPI_Allgatherv(hostBuffer + first * rowWidth, counts[rank], MPI_FLOAT,
+                   hostBuffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceBuffer, hostBuffer, n * rowWidth * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+}

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    val_t* exchangeBuffer = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&exchangeBuffer, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherDeviceFloatRows(dRadB + t * n, exchangeBuffer, 1, n, rank, ranks);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(exchangeBuffer));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }

+}
+
+void gatherRows(void* buffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+        size_t first, rows;
+        rowPartition(n, r, ranks, first, rows);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * rowWidth);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows;
+    rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, datatype, buffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+// Stage collectives through page-locked host memory.  This keeps the program
+// portable across standard MPI installations while CUDA still owns all dense
+// arithmetic; accelerator-aware MPI implementations can map this buffer for
+// their fast host/device transfer path.
+void gatherDeviceFloatRows(val_t* deviceBuffer, val_t* hostBuffer, size_t rowWidth, size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+        size_t first, rows; rowPartition(n, r, ranks, first, rows);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * rowWidth);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * rowWidth);
+    }
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(n, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostBuffer + first * rowWidth, deviceBuffer + first * rowWidth,
+                          rows * rowWidth * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    MPI_Allgatherv(hostBuffer + first * rowWidth, counts[rank], MPI_FLOAT,
+                   hostBuffer, counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceBuffer, hostBuffer, n * rowWidth * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+}
+

+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                  const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                  size_t n, size_t time, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        const int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (i != j && static_cast<int>(time) >= delay) {
+            const val_t k = kij[i * n + j];
+            if (k > ZERO) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], ONE) * radB[(time - delay) * n + j];
         }
+    }
+    extern __shared__ val_t partial[];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) radB[time * n + i] = rho[i] * partial[0] + radE[time * n + i];
+}
 
-        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
-            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t first, size_t rows) {
+    const size_t localI = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = first + localI;
+    if (localI >= rows || threadIdx.x != 0) return;
+    val_t maximum = ZERO; size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt) sum += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maximum) { maximum = sum; best = lag; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (rank == 0) printf("Running CUDA wave propagation simulation with MPI exchange...\n");
+    size_t first, rows; rowPartition(state.numTriangles, rank, ranks, first, rows);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, matrixBytes = n * n * sizeof(val_t), timeBytes = n * state.numTimesteps * sizeof(val_t);
+    val_t *dKij, *dAreas, *dRho, *dRadE, *dRadB; int* dTau;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dKij, matrixBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dTau, n * n * sizeof(int)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dAreas, n * sizeof(val_t))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRho, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadE, timeBytes)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dRadB, timeBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dKij, state.kij.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dTau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dAreas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dRadE, state.radE.data(), timeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, timeBytes));
+    val_t* exchangeBuffer = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&exchangeBuffer, n * sizeof(val_t)));
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        if (rows != 0) {
+            propagationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(rows), threads, threads * sizeof(val_t)>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadE, dRadB, n, t, first, rows);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError()); CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
         }
+        gatherDeviceFloatRows(dRadB + t * n, exchangeBuffer, 1, n, rank, ranks);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(), dRadB, timeBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(exchangeBuffer));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dKij)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dTau)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dAreas)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRho)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadE)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRadB));
 }
